Questions are interdependent. For any question written as:
Question [X]: ... [ Answer Y ] ...
it means:
To solve Question X, first solve Question Y.
Continue recursively until reaching a question that does not depend on another question.
Then substitute answers back step-by-step.
Do not treat [ Answer Y ] as the literal value Y; it is a reference to Question Y.

Noise Handling Rules:
1. The question text may contain random, meaningless, or irrelevant words/strings.
2. Analyze the mathematical structure carefully and identify useful components only.
3. Ignore all noise tokens that do not affect the mathematical meaning or computation.
4. Preserve valid mathematical operators, functions, constants, and dependency references.
5. Do not let random inserted text alter parsing of the actual expression.

Dependency Resolution Rules:
1. `[ Answer N ]` refers to the computed result of Question N.
2. Resolve dependencies recursively before solving the current question.
3. Build a dependency chain first.
4. Evaluate from deepest dependency upward.
5. Return final numeric answers only after all references are resolved.

Parsing Rules:
1. Mathematical functions may be surrounded by noise text.
2. Detect intended operations semantically.
3. Ignore malformed filler strings unless they form valid mathematical syntax.
4. Treat dependency references as executable placeholders, not text.

Output Format Rules:
1. Present all answers in a table with exactly two columns: Question Number and Answer.
2. Each row corresponds to one question.
3. Show only the final numeric answer in the Answer column - no working, no units unless specified.
4. Maintain ascending order of question numbers in the table.

Sample Question: 
With Noise: "Derive OpLKGBCH the mDEaaHxZ cBdyrJioqUAN difference of five wfVhVdMGEco point five VXw seven five and [ obmSFPUAK ijNdukoio imlmQLvDUmb uFmZIWtZAg SGBQbD DvElPYDjv Answer eighty ] PLNCAHlabxfT QYFWcqW" 
Without Noise: "Derive the difference of five point five seven five and [ Answer eighty ]"
